f_s(h) = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-s} h_i (1 - h_{i+s}) for s>=0.
For s<0: f_s = ∑_{i=-s}^{n-1} h_i (1 - h_{i+s}) = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1+?} same.
Let's handle s from -(n-1) to n-1.

Gradient:
For s>0:
f_s = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-s} (h_i - h_i h_{i+s}).
∂/∂h_i:
- if i <= n-1-s: from term with h_i as first: 1 - h_{i+s}.
- if i >= s and i <= n-1: term with h_{i-s} second: -h_{i-s}.
So grad_s[i] = (i < n-s ? 1 - h[i+s] : 0) - (i >= s ? h[i-s] : 0).
For s=0: f0=∑ h_i(1-h_i), grad[i]=1-2h_i.
For s<0 let t=-s: f_{-t} = ∑_{i=t}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-t}).
∂:
if i >= t and i <= n-1: first term (i): 1 - h_i? wait term for i: h_i(1-h_{i-t}); derivative = 1 - h_{i-t}.
Second term: when i = j-t? term h_j(1-h_i) derivative wrt h_i = -h_j for j=i+t. valid if i < n-t. So grad_{-t}[i] = (i>=t? 1 - h[i-t]:0) - (i < n - t ? h[i+t]:0). Good.

def palindrome_score(y, H):
  z = 1 - 2*y  (int8 maybe)
  zf = np.r_[z, z[::-1]]
  c = np.correlate(zf, zf, mode='full')
  lags_abs = np.abs(np.arange(1,N)- (N-1))
  L = N - lags_abs
  val = (L - c) / 4.0  # positive lag and negative; symmetric same
  return int(np.max(val[1:])?) Actually c[0] lag -N+1, central. Need exclude central index N-1. val = (N - abs(lag) - c)/4. score = max val excluding central. Since c symmetric, max around both sides. But val may not integer due to float; use np.rint.

For each pair u<v in P:
    d = v-u
    diff[d-1] += 4*z[u]*z[v]

Let's implement a random Markov sampler:
def generate_markov_y(H, k, q, seed):
    y = zeros H
    state = rng.randint(2)
    y[0]=state
    for i in 1..H-1:
       if rng.rand()<q: state=1-state
       y[i]=state
    # adjust sum
    ones=y.sum()
    if ones>k: flip random ones to0 until ones=k
    elif ones<k: flip random zeros to1
    return y
This may break Markov if few mismatches; if sum variance O(H q) small? For q~0.02,H128, ones mean64 (if start stationary?) variance? Markov covariance; std maybe sqrt(H/(4q))? For q small, std large? Stationary p=1/2, autocorr e^{-2q t}; variance of count ≈ (1/4)H(1+2∑ρ) = (1/4)H(1+ 2/(e^{2q}-1)?) For q=0.02, effective run 50, std ~sqrt(128/4*(1+2*1/(0.0404)))~sqrt(32*50)=40. Could deviate a lot. Adjusting many bits destructive. Instead do exact conditioning via run lengths.

    d) Block with noise: central/edge blocks plus random swaps.
  - For each start, run hill_climb_pal (best improvement) until no improvement or time slice. Keep best.
  - If local minima, perform random perturbation: swap a random number (e.g., 1-10) of y bits preserving sum, then hill_climb again. Use time.

def rebuild_fd(p, zf, Fdiff):

Projection onto sum exactly: 
def project_classification(y, target):
   # target n/2
   # bisection tau
   lo = y.min() - 1; hi = y.max() + 1
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      val=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
      if val > target: lo=mid else hi=mid
   return np.clip(y-mid,0,1)

def hill_climb_pal(y, H, deadline, max_scans=100000):
    N=2*H; k=H//2
    y = y.copy().astype(np.uint8)
    # ensure sum k; if not, adjust? assume
    zfull = ...
    Cpos = autocorr_pos(zfull,N)
    Fdiff = np.empty((N,N-1), np.int32)
    for p in range(N): rebuild_fd(p,zfull,Fdiff)
    Lpre = N - np.arange(1,N, dtype=int32)
    base_val = (Lpre - Cpos) // 4
    M = int(base_val.max())
    mirror = N - 1 - np.arange(H)
    ones = np.where(y==1)[0]
    zeros = np.where(y==0)[0]
    diff_buf = np.empty(N-1,np.int32); best_diff=np.empty(N-1,np.int32)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       best_m=M; best_a=-1; best_b=-1
       # create local lists maybe
       ones_list=ones.tolist(); zeros_list=zeros.tolist()
       for a in ones_list:
          ma=mirror[a]
          F1=Fdiff[a]; F2=Fdiff[ma]
          z1=zfull[a];? For corrections we need signs. But signs may be int16; use int.
          for b in zeros_list:
             mb=mirror[b]
             diff_buf[:] = F1
             diff_buf += Fdiff[b]
             diff_buf += Fdiff[mb]
             diff_buf += F2
             # corrections among P = [a,ma,b,mb]
             # unrolled:
             pa=a; pb=ma; pc=b; pd=mb
             sa=zfull[pa]; sb=zfull[pb]; sc=zfull[pc]; sd=zfull[pd]
             # pairs pa-pb,pa-pc,pa-pd,pb-pc,pb-pd,pc-pd
             d=abs(pa-pb); diff_buf[d-1] += 4*sa*sb
             ...
             # If any candidate val >= M? Since we need max < M. Could compute max.
             # Quick filter: if diff has very negative? No.
             m_new = int((base_val - diff_buf // 4).max())
             if m_new < best_m:
                 best_m=m_new; best_a=a; best_b=b; np.copyto(best_diff,diff_buf)
       if best_a<0:
           break
       # apply
       P=(best_a,mirror[best_a],best_b,mirror[best_b])
       for p in P: zfull[p] = -zfull[p]
       y[best_a]=0; y[best_b]=1
       Cpos += best_diff
       base_val -= best_diff // 4  # exact
       M=best_m
       # update ones/zeros arrays: remove best_a from ones, add best_b; etc
       # Use sets? For speed, maintain Python lists and swap?
       # We can update by filtering each improvement: ones = ones[ones != best_a] etc. Number improvements maybe not huge; okay.
       ones = np.delete(ones, np.where(ones==best_a)[0]); etc. Or use sets of ints and convert to list each scan. Since H small, use Python sets.
Better maintain ones_set=set(ones.tolist()), zeros_set. For scan, ones_list=sorted(ones_set) or list (unsorted). Order doesn't matter. After move: ones_set.remove(best_a); ones_set.add(best_b); zeros_set.remove(best_b); zeros_set.add(best_a).
       Update Fdiff affected: affected = set()
       for p in P:
          affected.add(p)
          if p>0: affected.add(p-1)
          if p<N-1: affected.add(p+1)
       for p in affected: rebuild_fd(p,zfull,Fdiff)
    # return y,M
    return y,M

Line search:
def smooth_polish(h,n,deadline, alpha_start=50, max_iter=2000):
   A,B=build_shift_pairs(n)
   h0=project_feasible(h,n)
   best=compute_raw(h0)
   cur_h=h0.copy(); best_raw=best
   alphas = [10,20,50,100,200,500,1000]
   for alpha in ... while time:
      lr=0.05
      for it in range(200) if time:
          f = compute_f_vec(cur_h,A,B)
          fmax=f.max()
          if not finite...
          # if fmax close to best? 
          sh=alpha*(f-fmax); w=np.exp(sh); w/=w.sum()
          # active threshold
          w[w<1e-9]=0; if sum=0 continue
          g=zeros
          loop shifts where w>0...
          # normalize? optional
          for trial in [lr, lr*0.5, lr*0.25,...]:
              y = cur_h - trial*g
              h_try=project_feasible(y,n)
              raw=compute_raw(h_try,n)
              if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                   cur_h=h_try; best_raw=raw; update best_h; break
          else:
              lr *= 0.5
              if lr<1e-5: break
   return best_h,best_raw

Pal initial from parent:
def make_pal_from_parent(parent,n_parent,N,H,k,seed):
   x0=np.linspace(0,2,n_parent); xp=np.linspace(0,2,N)
   full=np.interp(xp,x0,parent)
   sym=(full+full[::-1])*0.5
   val=sym[:H]
   # choose k largest; if ties random. Use argsort.
   idx=np.argsort(val)[::-1]
   y=np.zeros(H,np.uint8); y[idx[:k]]=1
   return y
But if parent is 1D length maybe n0; if n0=N, symmetric can violate left-right due to parent domain? OK.

Markov sampler:
def sample_balanced_markov(H,k,q,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   # P[rem][state][r]
   maxr=k
   P = np.zeros((H+1,2,k+1), dtype=float)
   P[0,0,0]=1; P[0,1,0]=1
   for rem in range(1,H+1):
       for st in (0,1):
           # stay
           # P[rem,st,r] = (1-q)*P[rem-1,st,r-st] + q*P[rem-1,1-st,r-(1-st)]
           stay = P[rem-1,st]
           flip = P[rem-1,1-st]
           if st==0:
              # r from stay: r; from flip: r-1
              arr = np.zeros(k+1)
              arr[0] = (1-q)*P[rem-1,0,0] + q*0? Wait if flip next=1 adds one: for r=0 only stay (r=0) possible.
              # easier loop r 0..k
              for r in range(0,k+1):
                  val=0.0
                  # stay next st: contributes r_st=st? st=0 no add
                  if r >= st:
                      val += (1-q)*P[rem-1,st,r-st]
                  # flip next=1-st: add fs=1-st
                  fs=1-st
                  if r >= fs:
                      val += q*P[rem-1,1-st,r-fs]
                  P[rem,st,r]=val
   # Double loop 128*2*65=16k, okay.
   # Choose last state initial? To make stationary, choose last rng 0/1. ones=0
   last = rng.randint(2)
   ones=0
   y=np.zeros(H,np.uint8)
   for pos in range(H):
       rem=H-pos
       r=k-ones
       # candidate 0 and1
       p0=0.0; p1=0.0
       # prob to choose 0:
       prob0 = (1-q if last==0 else q)
       if r >= 0:
          p0 = prob0 * P[rem-1,0,r]
       prob1 = (q if last==0 else 1-q)
       if r >= 1:
          p1 = prob1 * P[rem-1,1,r-1]
       total=p0+p1
       if total <=0 or not finite: fallback choose to satisfy
       elif rng.rand()*total < p0:
          s=0
       else:
          s=1
       y[pos]=s; last=s; ones += s
   # Ensure sum k; if not (due numerical), adjust (should)
   if ones != k:
       # random flip to fix
       if ones>k: flip random ones to0
       else flip zeros to1
   return y

Let's test non-pal update formula thoroughly.
Definitions:
z_i = 1 if h_i=1, -1 if0; sum z=0.
C_s = ∑_{i=0}^{N-s-1} z_i z_{i+s}; s=1..N-1.
D_s = (L_s - C_s)/2, L_s=N-s.
P_t=∑_{i=0}^{t-1} z_i, P_0=0,P_N=0.
B_s = (P_s + P_{N-s})/2.

Rebuild_fd for int16:
def rebuild_fd(p, z, Fdiff):
    row = Fdiff[p]
    row[:] = 0
    sp = z[p]
    if p > 0:
        # z[p-1::-1] int16; -2*sp * ... -> int64; row[:p] += ... (casts)
        row[:p] += (-2 * sp) * z[p-1::-1]
    if p + 1 < len(z):
        row[:len(z)-1-p] += (-2 * sp) * z[p+1:]
Need ensure row slice lengths match: suffix length len(z)-1-p, z[p+1:] length N-1-p. Good.

Potential overlap prefix/suffix addition: row[:p] and row[:N-1-p] may overlap; adding both via += works. But the second assignment after first modifies row in overlap, adding. Good.

Build flipB:
def build_flipB_full(z,N,flipB):
   s_idx = np.arange(1,N, dtype=np.int16)
   for p in range(N):
       A = s_idx > p
       B = s_idx < (N - p)
       flipB[p] = (-z[p]) * (A + B).astype(np.int16)
Check p=0: A true all, B true all (s<N) => 2; sign -z0. Good. p=N-1: A none, B s<1? true for s? N-p=1, s<1 none; so zero? Flipping last position affects B_s? B_s = (P_s+P_{N-s})/2. If p=N-1, P_t changes for t>N-1 none within 1..N-1; N-s > N-1? s<1 none. So no effect. Good.

Pseudo:
def binary_hill_climb(zinit, N, deadline):
    z = zinit.copy().astype(np.int16)
    # ensure sum zero? assume
    P = np.zeros(N+1, np.int32); P[1:] = np.cumsum(z)
    Lpre = N - np.arange(1,N,dtype=np.int32)
    Cpos = np.empty(N-1,np.int32)
    for s in range(1,N): Cpos[s-1] = np.dot(z[:N-s].astype(np.int32), z[s:].astype(np.int32))
    Fdiff = np.empty((N,N-1),np.int16)
    for p in range(N): rebuild_fd(p,z,Fdiff)
    flipB = np.empty((N,N-1),np.int16)
    sidx = np.arange(1,N,dtype=np.int16)
    for p in range(N): ...
    S = Lpre - Cpos
    B = (P[1:N] + P[N-1:0:-1]) // 2
    val2 = S + 2*np.abs(B)
    M = int(val2.max() // 2)
    ones = set(np.where(z==1)[0].tolist()); zeros=set(np.where(z==-1)[0].tolist())
    bufC=np.empty(N-1,np.int32); Bbuf=np.empty(N-1,np.int32); valbuf=np.empty(N-1,np.int32)
    bestC=np.empty(N-1,np.int32); bestB=np.empty(N-1,np.int32)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       best_m=M; best_a=-1
       time_check...
       for a in list(ones): # set iteration unstable? we won't modify during scan. list ok.
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          Fa=Fdiff[a]
          for b in list(zeros)? That creates many lists. Use zeros_list = list(zeros) outside. 
          for b in zeros_list:
             d=abs(a-b)
             bufC[:] = Fa
             bufC += Fdiff[b]
             bufC[d-1] -= 4
             Bbuf[:] = flipB[a]
             Bbuf += flipB[b]
             valbuf[:] = S
             valbuf -= bufC
             valbuf += 2 * np.abs(Bbuf)
             m2 = int(valbuf.max())
             m = m2 // 2
             if m < best_m:
                best_m=m; best_a=a; best_b=b; np.copyto(bestC,bufC); np.copyto(bestB,Bbuf)
       if best_a<0: break
       # apply
       b=best_b
       z[a] = -z[a]; z[b] = -z[b]
       if a < b: P[a+1:b+1] -= 2
       else: P[b+1:a+1] += 2
       Cpos += bestC; S -= bestC
       B += bestB
       # val2 recomputed for M? We use M=best_m. But for next candidate valbuf uses S and B directly, no val2_base needed. We don't need val2 array except initial? Candidate uses S,B. So no recompute val2.
       M = best_m
       affected={a,b,a-1,...}; rebuild
       flipB[a] *= -1; flipB[b] *= -1
       ones.remove(a); ones.add(b); zeros.remove(b); zeros.add(a)
    return z, M

    n0=len(parent); x0=np.linspace(0,2,n0); xp=np.linspace(0,2,N)
    h=np.interp(xp,x0,parent)

def perturb_z(z,N,seed,nswaps):
   z=z.copy()
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   ones = np.where(z==1)[0]; zeros=np.where(z==-1)[0]

def smooth_frag_obj_and_grad(h,A,B,alpha):
   f = np.empty(len(A))
   for i in range(len(A)):
      a=A[i]; b=B[i]
      f[i] = np.dot(h[a], 1-h[b])
   fmax = f.max()

Implement helper:
def compute_f_vector(h, A, B):
    f = np.empty(len(A), dtype=float)
    for i in range(len(A)):
        a=A[i]; b=B[i]
        f[i] = float(np.dot(h[a], 1.0 - h[b]))
    return f

def compute_gradient_smooth(h, A, B, w):
    g=np.zeros(len(h),float)
    for i in range(len(A)):
       wt=w[i]
       if wt < 1e-10: continue
       a=A[i]; b=B[i]
       if a[0] == b[0] and a.size == b.size: # diagonal shift 0, but for s=0 only a=b same array
           g[a] += wt*(1.0 - 2.0*h[a])
       else:
           g[a] += wt*(1.0 - h[b])
           g[b] -= wt*h[a]
    return g

For diagonal shift, A[0]=B[0]=arange(N). We need identify by maybe store `diag_idx` flag list.

Projection function project_sum.

def polish_smooth(h0,N,deadline,alphas=[20,50,100,200]):
    A,B,flags=build_pairs(N)
    cur=project_feasible(h0,N)
    best=cur.copy(); best_raw=compute_raw(cur,N)
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline-20: break
       lr=0.05? raw scale. We'll use trials [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,...]
       for it in range(300):
          if time>deadline-20: break
          f=compute_f_vector(cur,A,B)
          fmax=f.max()
          # if fmax close to best_raw? 
          sh=alpha*(f-fmax); sh-=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh); w/=w.sum()
          g=compute_gradient_smooth(cur,A,B,w)
          # Optional average active gradient finer? good.
          accepted=False
          for trial in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
              y=cur - trial*g
              hp=project_feasible(y,N)
              raw=compute_raw(hp,N)
              if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                  cur=hp; best=hp.copy(); best_raw=raw; accepted=True; break
          if not accepted:
              # try smaller 0.001 or break alpha
              y=cur - 0.001*g; hp=project_feasible...
              if raw < best_raw - ... accept else break
       # maybe recompute f? 
    return best,best_raw

Projection function efficient:
def project_feasible(y,N):
    target=N/2.0
    y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    # bisection on tau
    lo=y.min(); hi=y.max()
    # if sum y already target? 
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    vals=np.clip(y-mid,0,1)
    diff=target-vals.sum()
    if diff>0:
       room=1-vals
       # add to largest room first
       idx=np.argsort(room)[::-1]
       for i in idx:
          add=np.min(diff, room[i])
          vals[i]+=add; diff-=add
          if diff<=1e-14: break
    elif diff<0:
       idx=np.argsort(vals)[::-1] # vals descending? Need subtract from largest vals? Actually room lower = vals; to subtract, choose largest vals.
       for i in idx:
          sub=np.min(-diff, vals[i])
          vals[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
          if diff>=-1e-14: break
    return vals
This ensures sum. But bisection lo/hi should be such that for lo sum>=target, hi sum<=target. Set lo=y.min()-1 (all 1?), hi=y.max()+1. Use 60 iterations. Clip final.
We can optimize: Since target=N/2, projection to [0,1] and sum. Bisection okay.

Compute raw: `compute_raw(h,N)` = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full'))). The evaluation uses *dx. We'll return C=raw*2/N.

Precompute A,B:
def build_shift_pairs(N):
   A=[]; B=[]; flags=[]
   s0=np.arange(N)
   A.append(s0); B.append(s0); flags.append(True)
   for s in range(1,N):
       a=np.arange(N-s); b=a+s; A.append(a); B.append(b); flags.append(False)
   for s in range(1,N):
       t=s
       a=np.arange(t,N); b=a-t; A.append(a); B.append(b); flags.append(False)
   return A,B,flags

Let's detail sample DP implementation efficiently:
def sample_balanced_markov(N,k,q,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # P[rem][state][r]
    P = np.zeros((N+1,2,k+1), dtype=np.float64)
    P[0,0,0]=1.0; P[0,1,0]=1.0
    for rem in range(1,N+1):
        pr0 = P[rem-1,0]
        pr1 = P[rem-1,1]
        # state 0
        # stay next 0: P[rem-1,0,r]
        # flip next1: P[rem-1,1,r-1]
        # for r 0..k
        # We can vectorize:
        # P[rem,0,:]= (1-q)*P[rem-1,0,:]
        # flip: for r>=1 add q*P[rem-1,1,:-1]
        P[rem,0,:] = (1.0-q) * P[rem-1,0,:]
        if k >=1:
            P[rem,0,1:] += q * P[rem-1,1,:-1]
        # state 1
        # stay next1: add to r>=1: (1-q)*P[rem-1,1,r-1]
        # flip next0: q*P[rem-1,0,r]
        P[rem,1,:] = q * P[rem-1,0,:]
        if k >=1:
            P[rem,1,1:] += (1.0-q) * P[rem-1,1,:-1]
    # This is vectorized and correct.
    last = rng.randint(2)
    ones=0; y=np.zeros(N,np.int8)
    for pos in range(N):
       rem=N-pos; r=k-ones
       # candidate 0
       trans0 = (1-q) if last==0 else q
       p0 = trans0 * (P[rem-1,0,r] if 0<=r<=k else 0.0)
       trans1 = q if last==0 else (1-q)
       p1 = trans1 * (P[rem-1,1,r-1] if 1<=r<=k else 0.0)
       total=p0+p1
       if total <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(total):
          # fallback choose to meet target
          if r > rem: s=1 else? Actually if impossible, adjust.
          if r >= rem: s=1
          elif r <= 0: s=0
          else: s=1 if rng.rand()<r/rem else 0
       else:
          s = 0 if rng.rand()*total < p0 else 1
       y[pos]=s; last=s; ones+=s
    # adjust sum if needed
    if ones != k:
       if ones > k:
          idx=np.where(y==1)[0]
          for j in idx[np.argsort(rng.rand(len(idx)))[:ones-k]]: y[j]=0? Need flip ones-k to0.
       else:
          idx=np.where(y==0)[0]; choose k-ones to1.
    return y
Vectorized DP good. Be careful P[rem-1,0,:] length k+1; P[rem-1,1,:-1] length k; adding to P[rem,0,1:] length k. For state1, P[rem,1,:] = q*P[rem-1,0,:] (next0 no add) plus stay next1 shifted by +1: P[rem,1,1:] += (1-q)*P[rem-1,1,:-1]. Good.

Fallback if total zero: due to conditioning impossible? Shouldn't if P exact and k feasible. But use balanced filling.

Markov start full z: y binary 0/1; z=1-2*y. Ensure sum N/2; DP should.

For pal markov, N full length must 4m? H=N//2, k=H//2. Use sample_balanced_markov(H,k,q,seed) to get y length H. Then full z: yz=1-2*y; z=np.empty(N,int16); z[:H]=yz; z[H:]=yz[::-1]. This enforces sum z=0 because y sum H/2. Good.

Parent to pal z: h_interp; sym; yvals=sym[:H]; choose k=H/2 largest; y. If ties, random? Use argsort stable. z=1-2*y, concatenate reverse. For N not divisible by4, no pal.

Parent to z non-pal: choose top N/2 from h_interp. Maybe sort with rng tie-break? For deterministic seed. Use `idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]`. If h has many ties (binary parent), argsort arbitrary but stable? fine.

       best_h=hpar.copy(); best_c=cpar; best_n=npar
    # define update function? Cannot closure. We'll inline or helper returning tuple.

                # If M target achieved (c < target), maybe stop N? We can still explore but enough. If cval < 0.3808, we can reduce time? We'll continue a little to improve.

       # Also if best_h from N even sum exactly? If binary, idx recovers.

       if limit > time.time():
          zp,Mp=binary_hill_climb(zb,N,limit)
          hp=0.5*(zp.astype(np.float64)+1.0)
          cp=compute_c5(hp,N)
          if cp<best_c-1e-12: best_c=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); best_n=N

    # Fractional polish if time >60

       cpol = compute_c5(hpol, best_n)
       if cpol < best_c - 1e-12:
           best_c = cpol; best_h = hpol.copy(); best_n = best_n

    # Final ensure feasibility and recompute
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)

Potential performance issue: In `binary_hill_climb`, candidate loop `for bi,b in enumerate(zeros_list): ...` inside `for a in ones_list`. Use local variables for arrays and N to speed. Use `t=time.time` local.

Pseudo code with local variables:
def binary_hill_climb(zinit, N, deadline):
    z = zinit.astype(np.int16, copy=True)
    Lpre = N - np.arange(1, N, dtype=np.int32)
    P = np.zeros(N+1, dtype=np.int32)
    P[1:] = np.cumsum(z)
    Cpos = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    for s in range(1,N): Cpos[s-1] = int(z[:N-s] @ z[s:])
    Fdiff = np.empty((N,N-1), dtype=np.int16)
    for p in range(N): rebuild_fd(p,z,Fdiff)
    flipB = np.empty((N,N-1), dtype=np.int16)
    sidx = np.arange(1,N, dtype=np.int16)
    for p in range(N):
       flipB[p] = (-z[p]) * (((sidx > p) + (sidx < (N-p))).astype(np.int16))
    S = Lpre - Cpos
    B = (P[1:N] + P[N-1:0:-1]) // 2
    M = int((S + 2*np.abs(B)).max() // 2)
    ones = set(int(i) for i in np.where(z == 1)[0])  # generator okay? top-level? It's a loop, fine.
    zeros = set(int(i) for i in np.where(z == -1)[0])
    bufC = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    Bbuf = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    valbuf = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    bestC = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    bestB = np.empty(N-1, dtype=np.int32)
    tt = time.time
    while tt() < deadline:
        ones_list = list(ones); zeros_list = list(zeros)
        best_m = M; best_a=-1; best_b=-1
        overrun = False
        for a in ones_list:
            if tt() >= deadline: overrun=True; break
            Fa = Fdiff[a]
            Fba = flipB[a]  # for Bbuf base
            for bi,b in enumerate(zeros_list):
                if (bi & 0x7f) == 0 and tt() >= deadline:
                    overrun=True; break
                d = a - b
                if d < 0: d = -d
                bufC[:] = Fa
                bufC += Fdiff[b]
                bufC[d-1] -= 4
                Bbuf[:] = Fba
                Bbuf += flipB[b]
                valbuf[:] = S
                valbuf -= bufC
                valbuf += 2 * np.abs(Bbuf)
                vmax = int(valbuf.max())
                m = vmax // 2
                if m < best_m:
                    best_m = m; best_a=a; best_b=b
                    np.copyto(bestC, bufC)
                    np.copyto(bestB, Bbuf)
                if overrun: break
            if overrun: break
        if best_a >= 0:
            a=best_a; b=best_b
            z[a] = -z[a]; z[b] = -z[b]
            if a < b:
                P[a+1:b+1] -= 2
            else:
                P[b+1:a+1] += 2
            Cpos += bestC
            S = Lpre - Cpos
            B = (P[1:N] + P[N-1:0:-1]) // 2
            # Fdiff affected
            aff = [a,b]
            if a>0: aff.append(a-1)
            if a<N-1: aff.append(a+1)
            if b>0: aff.append(b-1)
            if b<N-1: aff.append(b+1)
            # unique? can rebuild duplicates okay
            for p in aff: rebuild_fd(p,z,Fdiff)
            flipB[a] = -flipB[a]
            flipB[b] = -flipB[b]
            ones.remove(a); ones.add(b)
            zeros.remove(b); zeros.add(a)
            M = int((S + 2*np.abs(B)).max() // 2)
            if tt() >= deadline: break
            continue
        if overrun: break
        break
    return z, M

Note: `Fba = flipB[a]` is a view. We set Bbuf[:] = Fba then Bbuf += flipB[b]. Good. But if a changes within scan? Fdiff/flipB not changed. Good.

If `best_m` remains M but a move with same M not accepted. Could there be move that keeps M but leads to better later? Best-improvement avoids; may be stuck. We can after no improvement do random perturbation outside. Good.

But to be safe use compute_c5. compute_c5 for binary h int float will give exact? dx * int. minor float. Good.

Sampling last=0, pos0 rem2,r1: p0=stay0 (0.5)*P[1,0,1]=0.25; p1=flip1(0.5)*P[1,1,0]=0.25; chooses 0/1. pos1 determined to meet sum. Good.

if ones != k:
    if ones > k:
       ones_idx=np.where(y==1)[0]
       flip = rng.choice(ones_idx, size=ones-k, replace=False)
       y[flip]=0
    else:
       zeros_idx=np.where(y==0)[0]
       flip = rng.choice(zeros_idx, size=k-ones, replace=False)
       y[flip]=1
This preserves length.

Now, parent_to_pal_z: Need if N%4 !=0 return None.
def parent_to_pal_z(parent,N,seed):
    if N % 4 !=0 or len(parent)==0: return None
    H=N//2; k=H//2
    x0=np.linspace(0,2,len(parent)); xp=np.linspace(0,2,N)
    full=np.interp(xp,x0,parent)
    sym=(full+full[::-1])*0.5
    vals=sym[:H]
    # to encourage exact pal, choose top k; tie break random
    # Add tiny noise? Not needed.
    idx=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    y=np.zeros(H,np.int8); y[idx[:k]]=1
    z=1-2*y
    out=np.empty(N,np.int16); out[:H]=z; out[H:]=z[::-1]
    return out

Parent_to_z maybe if parent sum not exact; clip:
def parent_to_z(parent,N,seed):
    x0=np.linspace(0,2,len(parent)); xp=np.linspace(0,2,N)
    h=np.interp(xp,x0,parent)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    z=np.full(N,-1,np.int16); z[idx[:N//2]]=1
    return z

Could also use random tie-breaking: h += rng tiny? But argsort deterministic. For parent with many equal values, top set arbitrary; local search fixes. We can add small noise: `h = h + 1e-6*np.random.RandomState(seed).rand(N)` to break ties. Good.

Perturb_z:
def perturb_z(z,N,seed,nswaps):
    out=z.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones=set(np.where(out==1)[0].tolist()); zeros=set(np.where(out==-1)[0].tolist())
    for _ in range(nswaps):
       if not ones or not zeros: break
       a_list=list(ones); b_list=list(zeros)
       a=a_list[rng.randint(len(a_list))]; b=b_list[rng.randint(len(b_list))]
       out[a]=-1; out[b]=1
       ones.discard(a); ones.add(b); zeros.discard(b); zeros.add(a)
    return out
This creates lists each swap; nswaps small.

om = 1.0 - h

Gradient: for diagonal i=0: g += wt*(1-2h) on a; for others g[a] += wt*(1-h[b]) = wt*om[b]? Since om[b]=1-h[b]. Need h[a]. So:
om=1-h
for i: if i==0: g[a] += wt*(1-2*h[a]); else: g[a] += wt*om[b]; g[b] -= wt*h[a]
This is fine.

Polish function:
def polish_smooth(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    A,B=build_shift_pairs(n)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best=h.copy(); best_raw=compute_raw(best,n)
    if time.time() > deadline: return best,best_raw
    t0=time.time()
    alphas=[20,50,100,200]
    trials=[0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
        lr_iter=0
        for it in range(300):
            if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
            f=compute_f_vector(h,A,B)
            fmax=f.max()
            if not np.isfinite(fmax): break
            # if fmax close to best_raw? It is raw max (not smooth). best_raw from correlate maybe same. 
            # If no active? 
            sh=alpha*(f-fmax); sh -= sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh); wsum=w.sum(); w/=wsum
            g=compute_gradient_smooth(h,A,B,w)
            accepted=False
            # normalize gradient by its max to choose step? raw scale
            gmax=np.abs(g).max()
            if gmax ==0: break
            # We can scale g by 1/gmax? Then step sizes in gradient norm.
            g = g / gmax
            for trial in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
                y = h - trial*g
                hp = project_feasible(y,n)
                raw = compute_raw(hp,n)
                if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                    h=hp; best=hp.copy(); best_raw=raw; accepted=True; break
            if not accepted:
                break
    return best,best_raw

Scaling gradient by gmax makes step dimensionless; trial 0.5 means change up to 0.5 in h. Good. But if gmax small, scaling amplifies noise; but gmax likely O(1). If gmax <1e-8 break.

Line search uses best_raw from correlate; h current may have raw slightly > best? We only update best on improvement; h=best. Good.

If no improvement at alpha, try next alpha? If g zero break. Could also reduce trial smaller; we include 0.005. If none, break.

Potential issue: Smooth objective gradient of fmax-based soft max; line search optimizes raw not smooth. Fine.

`project_feasible` may be called many times; bisection 50 iterations each. In polish, 7 trials*300*4 ~8400 projections, each 50*250=12.5k ->100M ops, too high! Need optimize projection. We can make projection faster using sorted order or simple while? Since h near [0,1] and step small, we can use a faster projection:
- Clip to [0,1]
- Calculate diff = target - sum
- If abs(diff)<1e-12 return
- If diff>0, add diff proportionally to room (1-h) but clip. This can be done iterative; maybe 2 iterations enough near feasible. Since previous h feasible and step small, diff small. We can do:
   h=np.clip(y,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   for _ in range(50):
      if diff>0:
         room=1-h
         s=room.sum()
         if s<1e-14: break
         h += diff/s*room
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
         diff=target-h.sum()
      else:
         s=h.sum()
         if s<1e-14: break
         h += diff/s*h  # subtract proportionally
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
         diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
   # final adjust one element
This is like previous parent. For small steps, converges in few iterations. For large trial 0.5, may need more but still n=256. Use 20 iterations. Fast. Implement top-level project_feasible with this adaptive method, no bisection. Since step y=h - trial*g with g components ±1, trial<=0.5, y within [-0.5,1.5]; projection to box then balanced. Iterative proportional clipping likely fast. We can fallback to bisection if not converge after 30.

Pseudo:
def project_feasible(y,n):
    target=n*0.5
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(y,float),0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
           room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
           if s<1e-14: break
           h = h + (diff/s)*room
           h[h>1]=1.0
       else:
           s=h.sum()
           if s<1e-14: break
           h = h + (diff/s)*h
           h[h<0]=0.0
    # exact adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>1e-12:
       idx=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
       for i in idx:
           add=min(diff,1-h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add; if diff<=1e-14 break
    elif diff<-1e-12:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       for i in idx:
           sub=min(-diff,h[i]); h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub; if diff>=-1e-14 break
    return h

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def compute_raw(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c))


def project_feasible(y, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(y, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    for _ in range(120):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
            np.minimum(h, 1.0, out=h)
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
            np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)

    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 1e-12:
        order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
        for i in order:
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[i] += add
            diff -= add
            if diff <= 1e-14:
                break
    elif diff < -1e-12:
        order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
        for i in order:
            sub = min(-diff, h[i])
            if sub <= 0.0:
                break
            h[i] -= sub
            diff += sub
            if diff >= -1e-14:
                break

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def ensure_z_balanced(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.int16).copy()
    target = n // 2
    ones = int(np.sum(z == 1))
    if ones > target:
        idx = np.where(z == 1)[0]
        z[idx[: ones - target]] = -1
    elif ones < target:
        idx = np.where(z == -1)[0]
        z[idx[: target - ones]] = 1
    return z


def rebuild_fd(p, z, Fdiff):
    row = Fdiff[p]
    row[:] = 0
    n = len(z)
    sp = int(z[p])
    if p > 0:
        row[:p] += (-2 * sp) * z[p - 1::-1]
    if p + 1 < n:
        row[:n - 1 - p] += (-2 * sp) * z[p + 1:]


def binary_hill_climb(zinit, n, deadline):
    z = ensure_z_balanced(zinit, n)
    n = int(len(z))

    lpre = n - np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.int32)
    prefix = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.int32)
    prefix[1:] = np.cumsum(z, dtype=np.int32)

    cpos = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    for s in range(1, n):
        cpos[s - 1] = int(z[:n - s] @ z[s:])

    fdiff = np.empty((n, n - 1), dtype=np.int16)
    for p in range(n):
        rebuild_fd(p, z, fdiff)

    flip_b = np.empty((n, n - 1), dtype=np.int16)
    sentx = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.int16)
    for p in range(n):
        mask = (sentx > p) + (sentx < (n - p))
        flip_b[p] = (-z[p]) * mask.astype(np.int16)

    S = lpre - cpos
    B = (prefix[1:n] + prefix[n - 1:0:-1]) // 2
    M = int((S + 2 * np.abs(B)).max() // 4)

    ones = set(int(i) for i in np.where(z == 1)[0])
    zeros = set(int(i) for i in np.where(z == -1)[0])

    bufC = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    Bbuf = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    valbuf = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    bestC = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    bestB = np.empty(n - 1, dtype=np.int32)

    tt = time.time
    while tt() < deadline:
        ones_list = list(ones)
        zeros_list = list(zeros)
        best_m = M
        best_a = -1
        best_b = -1
        overrun = False

        for a in ones_list:
            if tt() >= deadline:
                overrun = True
                break
            Fa = fdiff[a]
            Fba = flip_b[a]
            for bi, b in enumerate(zeros_list):
                if (bi & 0x7f) == 0 and tt() >= deadline:
                    overrun = True
                    break

                d = a - b
                if d < 0:
                    d = -d

                bufC[:] = Fa
                bufC += fdiff[b]
                bufC[d - 1] -= 4

                Bbuf[:] = Fba
                Bbuf += flip_b[b]

                valbuf[:] = S
                valbuf -= bufC
                valbuf += 2 * np.abs(Bbuf)

                vmax = int(valbuf.max())
                mval = vmax // 4
                if mval < best_m:
                    best_m = mval
                    best_a = a
                    best_b = b
                    np.copyto(bestC, bufC)
                    np.copyto(bestB, Bbuf)

            if overrun:
                break

        if best_a >= 0:
            a = best_a
            b = best_b

            z[a] = -z[a]
            z[b] = -z[b]

            if a < b:
                prefix[a + 1:b + 1] -= 2
            else:
                prefix[b + 1:a + 1] += 2

            cpos += bestC
            S = lpre - cpos
            B = (prefix[1:n] + prefix[n - 1:0:-1]) // 2

            affected = [a, b]
            if a > 0:
                affected.append(a - 1)
            if a + 1 < n:
                affected.append(a + 1)
            if b > 0:
                affected.append(b - 1)
            if b + 1 < n:
                affected.append(b + 1)
            for p in affected:
                rebuild_fd(p, z, fdiff)

            flip_b[a] = -flip_b[a]
            flip_b[b] = -flip_b[b]

            ones.remove(a)
            ones.add(b)
            zeros.remove(b)
            zeros.add(a)

            M = int((S + 2 * np.abs(B)).max() // 4)

            if tt() >= deadline:
                break
            continue

        if overrun:
            break
        break

    return z, M


def build_shift_pairs(n):
    A = []
    B = []

    idx0 = np.arange(n)
    A.append(idx0)
    B.append(idx0)

    for s in range(1, n):
        a = np.arange(n - s)
        b = a + s
        A.append(a)
        B.append(b)

    for s in range(1, n):
        a = np.arange(s, n)
        b = a - s
        A.append(a)
        B.append(b)

    return A, B


def compute_f_vector(h, A, B):
    om = 1.0 - h
    f = np.empty(len(A), dtype=float)
    for i in range(len(A)):
        f[i] = float(np.dot(h[A[i]], om[B[i]]))
    return f


def compute_gradient_smooth(h, A, B, w):
    g = np.zeros(len(h), dtype=float)
    om = 1.0 - h
    for i in range(len(A)):
        wt = w[i]
        if wt < 1e-12:
            continue
        a = A[i]
        b = B[i]
        if i == 0:
            g[a] += wt * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[a])
        else:
            g[a] += wt * om[b]
            g[b] -= wt * h[a]
    return g


def polish_smooth(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    A, B = build_shift_pairs(n)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best = h.copy()
    best_raw = compute_raw(h, n)
    tt = time.time

    if tt() > deadline - 10:
        return best, best_raw

    alphas = [20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0]
    trials = [0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if tt() > deadline - 8:
            break
        for _ in range(60):
            if tt() > deadline - 5:
                break
            f = compute_f_vector(h, A, B)
            fmax = float(f.max())
            if not np.isfinite(fmax):
                break
            sh = alpha * (f - fmax)
            sh -= sh.max()
            w = np.exp(sh)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                break
            w /= ws
            g = compute_gradient_smooth(h, A, B, w)
            gmax = float(np.abs(g).max())
            if gmax <= 1e-12:
                break
            g = g / gmax
            accepted = False
            for trial in trials:
                y = h - trial * g
                hp = project_feasible(y, n)
                raw = compute_raw(hp, n)
                if raw < best_raw - 1e-10:
                    h = hp
                    best = hp.copy()
                    best_raw = raw
                    accepted = True
                    break
            if not accepted:
                break
    return best, best_raw


def sample_balanced_markov(n, k, q, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    P = np.zeros((n + 1, 2, k + 1), dtype=float)
    P[0, 0, 0] = 1.0
    P[0, 1, 0] = 1.0

    for rem in range(1, n + 1):
        P[rem, 0, :] = (1.0 - q) * P[rem - 1, 0, :]
        if k >= 1:
            P[rem, 0, 1:] += q * P[rem - 1, 1, :-1]
        P[rem, 1, :] = q * P[rem - 1, 0, :]
        if k >= 1:
            P[rem, 1, 1:] += (1.0 - q) * P[rem - 1, 1, :-1]

    last = int(rng.randint(2))
    ones = 0
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)

    for pos in range(n):
        rem = n - pos
        r = k - ones
        trans0 = (1.0 - q) if last == 0 else q
        p0 = trans0 * (P[rem - 1, 0, r] if 0 <= r <= k else 0.0)
        trans1 = q if last == 0 else (1.0 - q)
        p1 = trans1 * (P[rem - 1, 1, r - 1] if 1 <= r <= k else 0.0)
        total = p0 + p1

        if total <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(total):
            if r >= rem:
                s = 1
            elif r <= 0:
                s = 0
            else:
                s = 1 if rng.rand() < (r / float(rem)) else 0
        else:
            s = 0 if rng.rand() * total < p0 else 1

        y[pos] = s
        last = s
        ones += s

    if ones > k:
        idx = np.where(y == 1)[0]
        if idx.size:
            flip = rng.choice(idx, size=min(k - ones, idx.size), replace=False)
            y[flip] = 0
    elif ones < k:
        idx = np.where(y == 0)[0]
        if idx.size:
            flip = rng.choice(idx, size=min(k - ones, idx.size), replace=False)
            y[flip] = 1

    return y


def parent_to_z(parent, n, seed):
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(parent))
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h = np.interp(xp, x0, parent)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = h + 1e-7 * rng.rand(n)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    z = np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int16)
    z[idx[: n // 2]] = 1
    return z


def parent_to_pal_z(parent, n, seed):
    if n % 4 != 0:
        return None
    h = n // 2
    k = h // 2
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(parent))
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    full = np.interp(xp, x0, parent)
    sym = 0.5 * (full + full[::-1])
    vals = sym[:h]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    vals = vals + 1e-7 * rng.rand(h)
    idx = np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
    y = np.zeros(h, dtype=np.int8)
    y[idx[:k]] = 1
    z = (1 - 2 * y).astype(np.int16)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int16)
    out[:h] = z
    out[h:] = z[::-1]
    return out


def perturb_z(z, n, seed, nswaps):
    out = ensure_z_balanced(z, n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    ones = set(int(i) for i in np.where(out == 1)[0])
    zeros = set(int(i) for i in np.where(out == -1)[0])
    for _ in range(max(0, int(nswaps))):
        if not ones or not zeros:
            break
        a = list(ones)[int(rng.randint(len(ones)))]
        b = list(zeros)[int(rng.randint(len(zeros)))]
        out[a] = -1
        out[b] = 1
        ones.discard(a)
        ones.add(b)
        zeros.discard(b)
        zeros.add(a)
    return out


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    reserve = max(30.0, min(170.0, 0.13 * budget_s))
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n_parent = len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        n_parent = 0

    if parent is not None and n_parent > 0:
        hp = project_feasible(parent, n_parent)
        cp = compute_c5(hp, n_parent)
        best_h = hp.copy()
        best_c = cp
        best_n = n_parent

    n_list = [256, 264, 252, 272, 250, 248, 280]
    q_list = [0.008, 0.015, 0.025, 0.04, 0.06, 0.10, 0.15]

    for N in n_list:
        if time.time() > main_deadline - 8:
            break
        if N <= 0 or (N & 1):
            continue

        starts = []
        if parent is not None:
            starts.append(parent_to_z(parent, N, seed))
            pals = parent_to_pal_z(parent, N, seed)
            if pals is not None:
                starts.append(pals)

        for qi, q in enumerate(q_list):
            if time.time() > main_deadline - 5:
                break
            yfull = sample_balanced_markov(N, N // 2, q, seed + 11 + N + 31 * qi)
            starts.append((1 - 2 * yfull).astype(np.int16))
            if N % 4 == 0:
                ypal = sample_balanced_markov(N // 2, N // 4, q, seed + 1000 + N + 31 * qi)
                zpal = 1 - 2 * ypal
                zfull = np.empty(N, dtype=np.int16)
                zfull[:N // 2] = zpal
                zfull[N // 2:] = zpal[::-1]
                starts.append(zfull)

        if not starts:
            continue

        nleft = len(starts)
        for si, zstart in enumerate(starts):
            if time.time() > main_deadline - 5:
                break
            remaining = max(2.0, main_deadline - 2.0 - time.time())
            per = remaining / max(1, nleft - si)
            per = max(1.5, min(7.0, per))
            if best_c < 0.3805:
                per = max(0.8, min(3.0, per))
            limit = time.time() + per
            if limit > main_deadline - 2.0:
                limit = main_deadline - 2.0
            if limit <= time.time():
                break

            zbest, Mbest = binary_hill_climb(zstart, N, limit)
            hval = 0.5 * (zbest.astype(float) + 1.0)
            cval = compute_c5(hval, N)
            if cval < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cval
                best_h = hval.copy()
                best_n = N
                best_N_local = N
                best_z_local = zbest.copy()

            if cval < 0.3805 and time.time() < main_deadline - 20:
                lim2 = time.time() + min(12.0, max(2.0, main_deadline - 8.0 - time.time()))
                if lim2 > main_deadline - 2.0:
                    lim2 = main_deadline - 2.0
                if lim2 > time.time():
                    zbest2, Mbest2 = binary_hill_climb(zbest, N, lim2)
                    hval2 = 0.5 * (zbest2.astype(float) + 1.0)
                    cval2 = compute_c5(hval2, N)
                    if cval2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_c = cval2
                        best_h = hval2.copy()
                        best_n = N
                        best_z_local = zbest2.copy()
                        zbest = zbest2

                    if cval2 < 0.3805 and time.time() < main_deadline - 10:
                        zp = perturb_z(zbest, N, seed + 999 + si, 3)
                        lim3 = time.time() + min(8.0, max(2.0, main_deadline - 6.0 - time.time()))
                        if lim3 > main_deadline - 2.0:
                            lim3 = main_deadline - 2.0
                        if lim3 > time.time():
                            zp2, _ = binary_hill_climb(zp, N, lim3)
                            hp2 = 0.5 * (zp2.astype(float) + 1.0)
                            cp2 = compute_c5(hp2, N)
                            if cp2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                                best_c = cp2
                                best_h = hp2.copy()
                                best_n = N

    if best_h is None:
        N = 256
        y = sample_balanced_markov(N, N // 2, 0.05, seed)
        z = ensure_z_balanced(1 - 2 * y, N)
        best_h = 0.5 * (z.astype(float) + 1.0)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, N)
        best_n = N

    if time.time() < deadline - 40 and best_n > 0:
        hbest = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
        idx = np.argsort(hbest)[::-1]
        zb = np.full(best_n, -1, dtype=np.int16)
        zb[idx[: best_n // 2]] = 1
        zcur, _ = binary_hill_climb(zb, best_n, time.time() + max(3.0, min(90.0, deadline - 35.0 - time.time())))
        hcur = 0.5 * (zcur.astype(float) + 1.0)
        ccur = compute_c5(hcur, best_n)
        if ccur < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = ccur
            best_h = hcur.copy()

    if time.time() < deadline - 12:
        hpol = polish_smooth(best_h, best_n, deadline - 4.0, seed)
        cpol = compute_c5(hpol, best_n)
        if cpol < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = cpol
            best_h = hpol.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
